结果与成本

跨轮结果仅用于工程比较；不能把差值全部归因于模态。标准 MRR 包含无效回答零分。成本是实际输入＋输出 token，含图像进入语言模型的 token，不能直接等价为美元或运行时间。

展示口径

统一示例：AegisLab INC-058FC5DD18AD。选用已有报告中审查过的服务延迟案例，便于横向阅读；它是说明性样例，不能代表总体成功率。每个 arm 展示该病例的一份真实请求。T_FULL 无图；两个 RQ4.5 hybrid 的一次请求实际含两张图，均完整提供。

左右两栏按说明/证据拆分，是展示层整理。历史输入中的混合段、旧措辞和重复都原样保留；拆分字符位置可验证重建，完整请求顺序可在每节底部展开。历史措辞冲突没有被悄悄修正。

详细 Markdown 报告

T_FULL

完整强文本；所有诊断信息在文本中，无 dashboard。

这个 arm 怎样构造一次诊断

  1. 保留冻结的指标、Trace、日志、关系、窗口摘要及父子耗时配对，按原有组织写入文字。
  2. 附上完整候选、单位与统计读法，以及检查根因和下游症状的任务说明。
  3. 一次调用直接输出五个不同候选及简短理由；不发送图像。
总体 MRR 0.4846总 tokens 22,243.9 / 例本例 RR 1.0000本例 tokens 24,794
T_FULL 实际没有图像输入；这里不生成或冒充一张文本 dashboard。
Prompt：系统、任务、读图及输出要求
You are an expert Site Reliability Engineer performing Root Cause Analysis (RCA) for a microservice application deployed on a Kubernetes cluster.

A fault has occurred in the system. Identify the component where the fault originated, not the loudest downstream or co-located symptom.

Root causes may be services, pods, or infrastructure nodes. Service, pod, and node names are case-local numeric IDs: three digits identify a service, four digits identify a node, and five digits identify a pod. Return only IDs from the exhaustive candidate list.

Evidence semantics shared by every representation:
- M contains twelve selected entity/metric series with 64 equal relative-time bins, explicit missingness, source units, baseline, peak, signed robust deviation, and MET-Z pre/current statistics.
- R contains operation-level trace count, error, latency, and TRC-L exclusive-latency/count comparisons. Exclusive latency estimates local operation time after child-span duration is removed.
- L contains a readable Denum-inspired log-template graph. Each case-local LT ID binds a normalized template to an entity, relative bin, severity, multiplicity, and retained diagnostic numbers; LOG-R compares error-keyword and total-log rates.
- G contains incident-specific directed caller -> callee edges and telemetry-derived propagation onset. A -> B means A calls B; a symptom in A may originate in B, but this direction alone does not prove causality.
- Missing/null means no usable observation, never numeric zero. Times are relative to the observation-window start. Metric-z and trace-derived scales are not directly comparable.

Use the selected SIRCL* diagnostic discipline in M -> R -> L -> G order:
- MET-Z analyzer (M): compare regular/current means and standard deviations at the public trace-derived split; a mean shift over 3σ is a fluctuating metric. Use the full series and missingness to sanity-check the summary.
- TRC-L analyzer (R): compare baseline/fault call counts and exclusive-latency p95. dC and dX are bounded log2 fold changes; rank_score is the sum of their positive parts. Inclusive latency includes children, while exclusive latency is local time after subtracting child spans.
- LOG-R analyzer (L): compare per-service error-keyword and total-log rates before/after the same public split. The score surfaces new/error-rate bursts, newly appearing logs, and volume drops; inspect the readable Denum templates and diagnostic numbers before accepting it.
- G: use concrete caller -> callee edges and onset order to test whether a candidate is a plausible origin. A -> B means A calls B.

Before committing the final ranking, apply SIRCL*'s verify-and-revise procedure:
INITIAL: form a preliminary top-three ranking and identify the strongest evidence for top-1.
VERIFY: test top-1 with exactly two questions answerable from the supplied M/R/L/G evidence, including one possible contradiction or alternative origin.
REVISE: change the ranking if either check contradicts top-1; otherwise keep it.

Perform these checks within this single call, then return only the registered JSON object. Its reason must be one compact paragraph of at most three sentences that states the two verified checks and any decisive topology/temporal relation. Never include the literal working labels `INITIAL`, `VERIFY`, or `REVISE`, never reproduce the private working process, and never emit hidden chain-of-thought. Missing/null means absence, never numeric zero.

Output exactly one JSON object with this schema before reading the evidence below:
{"services":["candidate-id","candidate-id"],"reason":"brief evidence-grounded verification summary","confidence":"high|medium|low"}
The services list is ranked, contains at most five IDs, and uses only the exhaustive candidates supplied below.

Source reading: request-duration quantiles include the scope stated by their source; only explicit exclusive latency is local time after child spans. Independent quantiles must not be subtracted. Keep native units; do not infer conversions from a metric name. Resource gauges, accumulated counters, restart states and request counts have different meanings. Correlated metrics from one entity are not independent votes. Recovery does not rule out an earlier initiating fault. Observed order and calls/hosting alone do not prove origin. Bind every claim to its entity and metric or operation; cite MET/TRC/LOG/REL references where provided.


Literal reading and evidence citations: retain explicit source units; an unverified unit label does not authorize conversion. log2 ratios are not fold changes. Three-digit IDs denote services, four-digit IDs nodes, five-digit IDs pods; bind an instance through the actual relationship facts. Independent inclusive/exclusive p95 quantiles cannot be subtracted as paired durations. Binned observation order and anomaly-onset summaries do not certify causal order or fault origin. In the required reason, cite actual M/R/L/G field IDs and distinguish measured evidence from inference. Keep the required ranked top-five JSON schema.


Exact compact sequences: length/value_positions lists each value and its zero-based bin positions; a-b includes both endpoints. Every bin is present exactly once. Missing/null is no observation, never zero. Metric series references MET01.. bind the same entity, metric and source unit as their metadata.


G: calls A -> B means A calls B; hosts node -> pod and owns service -> pod are deployment relations, not causal assertions. Displayed onset is a relative observation time; displayed severity/rank are telemetry summaries.


Service names, pod names, and node names are represented by numeric IDs.


Common evidence; field=evidence_legends; entities=[]; bins=[]; unit=null; directed_edges="caller -> callee means caller invokes callee" entity_ids="Service names, pod names, and node names are represented by numeric IDs." relative_time="64 equal relative bins; null is missing" source_comparability="metric and trace anomaly scales are not directly comparable"


Additional observations use the same public reference/current intervals, in seconds from observation start. Medians describe measured samples; inclusive span duration includes child spans. Request counts deduplicate trace IDs and describe captured requests, not unobserved traffic. Calls and hosting are observed relations, not proof of failure propagation.


Additional component facts not already present above. Graph edges are [source,target,relation]; node type follows numeric ID length; isolated nodes are not displayed.


Reference operands AUX01..AUX06 are additional public metric series, supplied identically in all six arms. They use finite canonical samples' median in each of the same64 bins, without interpolation. Their native unit labels are unverified unless explicitly defined; do not infer conversions. Reference-only ridge models are validated on the full eligible public pool; displayed probes maximize disagreement only among qualified, in-support predictions. Selection uses no labels or previous model answers. Original12 metrics and R/L/G remain in text. A pool search is not a calibrated statistical test, causal discovery or a fault-response guarantee.


Full-window observations: metric series use their original time bins and native units. Extrema and first/last values describe the whole observation window; reference and current intervals retain their public definitions. Trace summaries compare reference/current exclusive p95, not inclusive duration. All original telemetry, additional operands, logs, relations and candidates remain in text.


Observed request pairs: all durations below are milliseconds, inclusive span durations. Each pair uses one recorded parent call and its actual direct child in the same request. One longest parent per request/operation, then its longest child per operation; absent children are not zero. Phases use the parent call and the public window. Counts cover eligible captured requests. Slow means strictly above that coordinate's reference p95; it is not a fault label. Associations and duration differences do not identify causal origin, waiting or local execution time.
Pair fields: [id,parent,parent_operation,child,child_operation,[parent_ref_p95,child_ref_p95],phases]. Phase: [name,n,counts[notSlow/notSlow,notSlow/slow,slow/notSlow,slow/slow]].


Continuous pair fields: [id,phases]; phase=[name,[[parent_p50,p95],[child_p50,p95]],conditional_child[[parent_slow,n,child_p50,p95]],sampled_pairs[[parent_ms,child_ms]]]. Each phase samples at most32 equally spaced parent-duration ranks, including endpoints; not all requests. The log1p(ms) presentation transform does not change these exact millisecond samples.


Output cardinality for this request: return exactly five distinct IDs from the supplied candidate list, ranked by plausibility. Additional ranks are alternatives, not claims of five simultaneous causes. Keep the same services, reason, and confidence JSON fields.

Based on the above, identify the root cause.

原请求顺序与全部文本：未经改写的完整 parts

System

You are an expert Site Reliability Engineer performing Root Cause Analysis (RCA) for a microservice application deployed on a Kubernetes cluster.

Part 0 · text

A fault has occurred in the system. Identify the component where the fault originated, not the loudest downstream or co-located symptom.

Root causes may be services, pods, or infrastructure nodes. Service, pod, and node names are case-local numeric IDs: three digits identify a service, four digits identify a node, and five digits identify a pod. Return only IDs from the exhaustive candidate list.

Evidence semantics shared by every representation:
- M contains twelve selected entity/metric series with 64 equal relative-time bins, explicit missingness, source units, baseline, peak, signed robust deviation, and MET-Z pre/current statistics.
- R contains operation-level trace count, error, latency, and TRC-L exclusive-latency/count comparisons. Exclusive latency estimates local operation time after child-span duration is removed.
- L contains a readable Denum-inspired log-template graph. Each case-local LT ID binds a normalized template to an entity, relative bin, severity, multiplicity, and retained diagnostic numbers; LOG-R compares error-keyword and total-log rates.
- G contains incident-specific directed caller -> callee edges and telemetry-derived propagation onset. A -> B means A calls B; a symptom in A may originate in B, but this direction alone does not prove causality.
- Missing/null means no usable observation, never numeric zero. Times are relative to the observation-window start. Metric-z and trace-derived scales are not directly comparable.

Use the selected SIRCL* diagnostic discipline in M -> R -> L -> G order:
- MET-Z analyzer (M): compare regular/current means and standard deviations at the public trace-derived split; a mean shift over 3σ is a fluctuating metric. Use the full series and missingness to sanity-check the summary.
- TRC-L analyzer (R): compare baseline/fault call counts and exclusive-latency p95. dC and dX are bounded log2 fold changes; rank_score is the sum of their positive parts. Inclusive latency includes children, while exclusive latency is local time after subtracting child spans.
- LOG-R analyzer (L): compare per-service error-keyword and total-log rates before/after the same public split. The score surfaces new/error-rate bursts, newly appearing logs, and volume drops; inspect the readable Denum templates and diagnostic numbers before accepting it.
- G: use concrete caller -> callee edges and onset order to test whether a candidate is a plausible origin. A -> B means A calls B.

Before committing the final ranking, apply SIRCL*'s verify-and-revise procedure:
INITIAL: form a preliminary top-three ranking and identify the strongest evidence for top-1.
VERIFY: test top-1 with exactly two questions answerable from the supplied M/R/L/G evidence, including one possible contradiction or alternative origin.
REVISE: change the ranking if either check contradicts top-1; otherwise keep it.

Perform these checks within this single call, then return only the registered JSON object. Its reason must be one compact paragraph of at most three sentences that states the two verified checks and any decisive topology/temporal relation. Never include the literal working labels `INITIAL`, `VERIFY`, or `REVISE`, never reproduce the private working process, and never emit hidden chain-of-thought. Missing/null means absence, never numeric zero.

Output exactly one JSON object with this schema before reading the evidence below:
{"services":["candidate-id","candidate-id"],"reason":"brief evidence-grounded verification summary","confidence":"high|medium|low"}
The services list is ranked, contains at most five IDs, and uses only the exhaustive candidates supplied below.

Part 1 · text

Source reading: request-duration quantiles include the scope stated by their source; only explicit exclusive latency is local time after child spans. Independent quantiles must not be subtracted. Keep native units; do not infer conversions from a metric name. Resource gauges, accumulated counters, restart states and request counts have different meanings. Correlated metrics from one entity are not independent votes. Recovery does not rule out an earlier initiating fault. Observed order and calls/hosting alone do not prove origin. Bind every claim to its entity and metric or operation; cite MET/TRC/LOG/REL references where provided.

Part 2 · text

Literal reading and evidence citations: retain explicit source units; an unverified unit label does not authorize conversion. log2 ratios are not fold changes. Three-digit IDs denote services, four-digit IDs nodes, five-digit IDs pods; bind an instance through the actual relationship facts. Independent inclusive/exclusive p95 quantiles cannot be subtracted as paired durations. Binned observation order and anomaly-onset summaries do not certify causal order or fault origin. In the required reason, cite actual M/R/L/G field IDs and distinguish measured evidence from inference. Keep the required ranked top-five JSON schema.

Part 3 · text

Exact compact sequences: length/value_positions lists each value and its zero-based bin positions; a-b includes both endpoints. Every bin is present exactly once. Missing/null is no observation, never zero. Metric series references MET01.. bind the same entity, metric and source unit as their metadata.

Part 4 · text

G: calls A -> B means A calls B; hosts node -> pod and owns service -> pod are deployment relations, not causal assertions. Displayed onset is a relative observation time; displayed severity/rank are telemetry summaries.

Part 12 · text

Observed request pairs: all durations below are milliseconds, inclusive span durations. Each pair uses one recorded parent call and its actual direct child in the same request. Prompt：系统、任务、读图及输出要求
You are an expert Site Reliability Engineer performing Root Cause Analysis (RCA) for a microservice application deployed on a Kubernetes cluster.

Identify the original faulty component, not the largest downstream or co-located symptom. Services use three-digit IDs, nodes four, and pods five. Rank exactly five distinct IDs from the exhaustive candidates; lower ranks are alternatives, not five simultaneous causes.

Use the existing SIRCL* discipline in M -> R -> L -> G order:
- M: twelve selected metric series have 64 equal relative-time bins, source units, baseline/peak, signed robust deviation and MET-Z statistics. Compare regular/current means and standard deviations at the public trace-derived split; a shift over 3 sigma is a fluctuating metric. Check the series and missingness against the summary.
- R: operation counts, errors and latency include TRC-L count/exclusive-p95 comparisons. dC/dX are bounded log2 changes; rank_score sums their positive parts. Inclusive duration includes children; explicitly exclusive duration estimates local time after child spans are removed.
- L: Denum-inspired LT template graph bind entity, relative bin, severity, multiplicity and diagnostic numbers. LOG-R compares error-keyword/total-log rates at the same split, surfacing new templates, error bursts and volume drops; check templates and numbers before accepting the score.
- G: directed caller -> callee edges and propagation onset test plausible origins. A symptom in caller A can originate in callee B. Calls, hosting and binned/onset order alone do not establish causality.

Missing/null is absent observation, never zero; times start at the observation window. Keep native units: names/unverified labels do not justify conversions. Metric-z and trace scales differ; log2 ratios are not fold changes. Respect each duration's stated scope; do not subtract independent inclusive/exclusive quantiles as paired durations. Gauges, accumulated counters, restart states and request counts differ. Correlated metrics of one entity are not independent votes. Recovery does not exclude an earlier initiating fault. Bind every claim to the correct entity, metric/operation and visible source ID; distinguish measurements from inference.

Within this single call, form a preliminary top-three, test top-1 with exactly two evidence-answerable checks including a contradiction or alternative origin, then revise if contradicted. Return only the JSON below. Its reason is one compact paragraph, at most three sentences, stating both checks and any decisive relationship. Do not reveal hidden working or output INITIAL/VERIFY/REVISE labels.

Part 2 · text

Identify the original faulty component, not the largest downstream or co-located symptom. Services use three-digit IDs, nodes four, and pods five. Rank exactly five distinct IDs from the exhaustive candidates; lower ranks are alternatives, not five simultaneous causes.

Use the existing SIRCL* discipline in M -> R -> L -> G order:
- M: twelve selected metric series have 64 equal relative-time bins, source units, baseline/peak, signed robust deviation and MET-Z statistics. Compare regular/current means and standard deviations at the public trace-derived split; a shift over 3 sigma is a fluctuating metric. Check the series and missingness against the summary.
- R: operation counts, errors and latency include TRC-L count/exclusive-p95 comparisons. dC/dX are bounded log2 changes; rank_score sums their positive parts. Inclusive duration includes children; explicitly exclusive duration estimates local time after child spans are removed.
- L: Denum-inspired LT template graph bind entity, relative bin, severity, multiplicity and diagnostic numbers. LOG-R compares error-keyword/total-log rates at the same split, surfacing new templates, error bursts and volume drops; check templates and numbers before accepting the score.
- G: directed caller -> callee edges and propagation onset test plausible origins. A symptom in caller A can originate in callee B. Calls, hosting and binned/onset order alone do not establish causality.

Missing/null is absent observation, never zero; times start at the observation window. Keep native units: names/unverified labels do not justify conversions. Metric-z and trace scales differ; log2 ratios are not fold changes. Respect each duration's stated scope; do not subtract independent inclusive/exclusive quantiles as paired durations. Gauges, accumulated counters, restart states and request counts differ. Correlated metrics of one entity are not independent votes. Recovery does not exclude an earlier initiating fault. Bind every claim to the correct entity, metric/operation and visible source ID; distinguish measurements from inference.

Within this single call, form a preliminary top-three, test top-1 with exactly two evidence-answerable checks including a contradiction or alternative origin, then revise if contradicted. Return only the JSON below. Its reason is one compact paragraph, at most three sentences, stating both checks and any decisive relationship. Do not reveal hidden working or output INITIAL/VERIFY/REVISE labels.

MARGINAL_FIVE

历史高分混合方案：完整文本＋重复呈现配对分布的原生边际图。

这个 arm 怎样构造一次诊断

  1. 保留完整 PAIR_FIVE 文字，包括已经存在的配对样本和统计。
  2. 按父调用两阶段 p95 耗时变化选择最多两个合格关系，每阶段最多展示 32 个真实配对。
  3. 将父、子各自的参考与当前耗时画成边际分布，再与完整文字一起输入。
  4. 输出固定五候选。FIVE 指输出数量；图像重复部分文本事实，并不替代那部分文字。

Prompt：系统、任务、读图及输出要求
You are an expert Site Reliability Engineer performing Root Cause Analysis (RCA) for a microservice application deployed on a Kubernetes cluster.

A fault has occurred in the system. Identify the component where the fault originated, not the loudest downstream or co-located symptom.

Root causes may be services, pods, or infrastructure nodes. Service, pod, and node names are case-local numeric IDs: three digits identify a service, four digits identify a node, and five digits identify a pod. Return only IDs from the exhaustive candidate list.

Evidence semantics shared by every representation:
- M contains twelve selected entity/metric series with 64 equal relative-time bins, explicit missingness, source units, baseline, peak, signed robust deviation, and MET-Z pre/current statistics.
- R contains operation-level trace count, error, latency, and TRC-L exclusive-latency/count comparisons. Exclusive latency estimates local operation time after child-span duration is removed.
- L contains a readable Denum-inspired log-template graph. Each case-local LT ID binds a normalized template to an entity, relative bin, severity, multiplicity, and retained diagnostic numbers; LOG-R compares error-keyword and total-log rates.
- G contains incident-specific directed caller -> callee edges and telemetry-derived propagation onset. A -> B means A calls B; a symptom in A may originate in B, but this direction alone does not prove causality.
- Missing/null means no usable observation, never numeric zero. Times are relative to the observation-window start. Metric-z and trace-derived scales are not directly comparable.

Use the selected SIRCL* diagnostic discipline in M -> R -> L -> G order:
- MET-Z analyzer (M): compare regular/current means and standard deviations at the public trace-derived split; a mean shift over 3σ is a fluctuating metric. Use the full series and missingness to sanity-check the summary.
- TRC-L analyzer (R): compare baseline/fault call counts and exclusive-latency p95. dC and dX are bounded log2 fold changes; rank_score is the sum of their positive parts. Inclusive latency includes children, while exclusive latency is local time after subtracting child spans.
- LOG-R analyzer (L): compare per-service error-keyword and total-log rates before/after the same public split. The score surfaces new/error-rate bursts, newly appearing logs, and volume drops; inspect the readable Denum templates and diagnostic numbers before accepting it.
- G: use concrete caller -> callee edges and onset order to test whether a candidate is a plausible origin. A -> B means A calls B.

Before committing the final ranking, apply SIRCL*'s verify-and-revise procedure:
INITIAL: form a preliminary top-three ranking and identify the strongest evidence for top-1.
VERIFY: test top-1 with exactly two questions answerable from the supplied M/R/L/G evidence, including one possible contradiction or alternative origin.
REVISE: change the ranking if either check contradicts top-1; otherwise keep it.

Perform these checks within this single call, then return only the registered JSON object. Its reason must be one compact paragraph of at most three sentences that states the two verified checks and any decisive topology/temporal relation. Never include the literal working labels `INITIAL`, `VERIFY`, or `REVISE`, never reproduce the private working process, and never emit hidden chain-of-thought. Missing/null means absence, never numeric zero.

Output exactly one JSON object with this schema before reading the evidence below:
{"services":["candidate-id","candidate-id"],"reason":"brief evidence-grounded verification summary","confidence":"high|medium|low"}
The services list is ranked, contains at most five IDs, and uses only the exhaustive candidates supplied below.

Source reading: request-duration quantiles include the scope stated by their source; only explicit exclusive latency is local time after child spans. Independent quantiles must not be subtracted. Keep native units; do not infer conversions from a metric name. Resource gauges, accumulated counters, restart states and request counts have different meanings. Correlated metrics from one entity are not independent votes. Recovery does not rule out an earlier initiating fault. Observed order and calls/hosting alone do not prove origin. Bind every claim to its entity and metric or operation; cite MET/TRC/LOG/REL references where provided.


Literal reading and evidence citations: retain explicit source units; an unverified unit label does not authorize conversion. log2 ratios are not fold changes. Three-digit IDs denote services, four-digit IDs nodes, five-digit IDs pods; bind an instance through the actual relationship facts. Independent inclusive/exclusive p95 quantiles cannot be subtracted as paired durations. Binned observation order and anomaly-onset summaries do not certify causal order or fault origin. In the required reason, cite actual M/R/L/G field IDs and distinguish measured evidence from inference. Keep the required ranked top-five JSON schema.


Exact compact sequences: length/value_positions lists each value and its zero-based bin positions; a-b includes both endpoints. Every bin is present exactly once. Missing/null is no observation, never zero. Metric series references MET01.. bind the same entity, metric and source unit as their metadata.


G: calls A -> B means A calls B; hosts node -> pod and owns service -> pod are deployment relations, not causal assertions. Displayed onset is a relative observation time; displayed severity/rank are telemetry summaries.


Service names, pod names, and node names are represented by numeric IDs.


Common evidence; field=evidence_legends; entities=[]; bins=[]; unit=null; directed_edges="caller -> callee means caller invokes callee" entity_ids="Service names, pod names, and node names are represented by numeric IDs." relative_time="64 equal relative bins; null is missing" source_comparability="metric and trace anomaly scales are not directly comparable"


Additional observations use the same public reference/current intervals, in seconds from observation start. Medians describe measured samples; inclusive span duration includes child spans. Request counts deduplicate trace IDs and describe captured requests, not unobserved traffic. Calls and hosting are observed relations, not proof of failure propagation.


Additional component facts not already present above. Graph edges are [source,target,relation]; node type follows numeric ID length; isolated nodes are not displayed.


Reference operands AUX01..AUX06 are additional public metric series, supplied identically in all six arms. They use finite canonical samples' median in each of the same64 bins, without interpolation. Their native unit labels are unverified unless explicitly defined; do not infer conversions. Reference-only ridge models are validated on the full eligible public pool; displayed probes maximize disagreement only among qualified, in-support predictions. Selection uses no labels or previous model answers. Original12 metrics and R/L/G remain in text. A pool search is not a calibrated statistical test, causal discovery or a fault-response guarantee.


Full-window observations: metric curves use their original time bins and independent linear native-unit axes. Extrema and first/last values describe the whole observation window; reference and current intervals retain their public definitions. Trace bars compare reference/current exclusive p95, not inclusive duration. Colors distinguish measurements, not root probability. All original telemetry, additional operands, logs, relations and candidates remain in text.


Observed request pairs: all durations below are milliseconds, inclusive span durations. Each pair uses one recorded parent call and its actual direct child in the same request. One longest parent per request/operation, then its longest child per operation; absent children are not zero. Phases use the parent call and the public window. Counts cover eligible captured requests. Slow means strictly above that coordinate's reference p95; it is not a fault label. Associations and duration differences do not identify causal origin, waiting or local execution time.
Pair fields: [id,parent,parent_operation,child,child_operation,[parent_ref_p95,child_ref_p95],phases]. Phase: [name,n,counts[notSlow/notSlow,notSlow/slow,slow/notSlow,slow/slow]].


Continuous pair fields: [id,phases]; phase=[name,[[parent_p50,p95],[child_p50,p95]],conditional_child[[parent_slow,n,child_p50,p95]],sampled_pairs[[parent_ms,child_ms]]]. Each phase samples at most32 equally spaced parent-duration ranks, including endpoints; not all requests. Joint and marginal graphs repeat these samples on the same log1p(ms) scale.


Output cardinality for this request: return exactly five distinct IDs from the supplied candidate list, ranked by plausibility. Additional ranks are alternatives, not claims of five simultaneous causes. Keep the same services, reason, and confidence JSON fields.

Based on the above, identify the root cause.

原请求顺序与全部文本：未经改写的完整 parts

System

You are an expert Site Reliability Engineer performing Root Cause Analysis (RCA) for a microservice application deployed on a Kubernetes cluster.

Part 0 · image

图像占位；对应上方实际 PNG。

Part 1 · text

A fault has occurred in the system. Identify the component where the fault originated, not the loudest downstream or co-located symptom.

Root causes may be services, pods, or infrastructure nodes. Service, pod, and node names are case-local numeric IDs: three digits identify a service, four digits identify a node, and five digits identify a pod. Return only IDs from the exhaustive candidate list.

Evidence semantics shared by every representation:
- M contains twelve selected entity/metric series with 64 equal relative-time bins, explicit missingness, source units, baseline, peak, signed robust deviation, and MET-Z pre/current statistics.
- R contains operation-level trace count, error, latency, and TRC-L exclusive-latency/count comparisons. Exclusive latency estimates local operation time after child-span duration is removed.
- L contains a readable Denum-inspired log-template graph. Each case-local LT ID binds a normalized template to an entity, relative bin, severity, multiplicity, and retained diagnostic numbers; LOG-R compares error-keyword and total-log rates.
- G contains incident-specific directed caller -> callee edges and telemetry-derived propagation onset. A -> B means A calls B; a symptom in A may originate in B, but this direction alone does not prove causality.
- Missing/null means no usable observation, never numeric zero. Times are relative to the observation-window start. Metric-z and trace-derived scales are not directly comparable.

Use the selected SIRCL* diagnostic discipline in M -> R -> L -> G order:
- MET-Z analyzer (M): compare regular/current means and standard deviations at the public trace-derived split; a mean shift over 3σ is a fluctuating metric. Use the full series and missingness to sanity-check the summary.
- TRC-L analyzer (R): compare baseline/fault call counts and exclusive-latency p95. dC and dX are bounded log2 fold changes; rank_score is the sum of their positive parts. Inclusive latency includes children, while exclusive latency is local time after subtracting child spans.
- LOG-R analyzer (L): compare per-service error-keyword and total-log rates before/after the same public split. The score surfaces new/error-rate bursts, newly appearing logs, and volume drops; inspect the readable Denum templates and diagnostic numbers before accepting it.
- G: use concrete caller -> callee edges and onset order to test whether a candidate is a plausible origin. A -> B means A calls B.

Before committing the final ranking, apply SIRCL*'s verify-and-revise procedure:
INITIAL: form a preliminary top-three ranking and identify the strongest evidence for top-1.
VERIFY: test top-1 with exactly two questions answerable from the supplied M/R/L/G evidence, including one possible contradiction or alternative origin.
REVISE: change the ranking if either check contradicts top-1; otherwise keep it.

Perform these checks within this single call, then return only the registered JSON object. Its reason must be one compact paragraph of at most three sentences that states the two verified checks and any decisive topology/temporal relation. Never include the literal working labels `INITIAL`, `VERIFY`, or `REVISE`, never reproduce the private working process, and never emit hidden chain-of-thought. Missing/null means absence, never numeric zero.

Output exactly one JSON object with this schema before reading the evidence below:
{"services":["candidate-id","candidate-id"],"reason":"brief evidence-grounded verification summary","confidence":"high|medium|low"}
The services list is ranked, contains at most five IDs, and uses only the exhaustive candidates supplied below.

Part 2 · text

Source reading: request-duration quantiles include the scope stated by their source; only explicit exclusive latency is local time after child spans. Independent quantiles must not be subtracted. Keep native units; do not infer conversions from a metric name. Resource gauges, accumulated counters, restart states and request counts have different meanings. Correlated metrics from one entity are not independent votes. Recovery does not rule out an earlier initiating fault. Observed order and calls/hosting alone do not prove origin. Bind every claim to its entity and metric or operation; cite MET/TRC/LOG/REL references where provided.

Part 3 · text

Literal reading and evidence citations: retain explicit source units; an unverified unit label does not authorize conversion. log2 ratios are not fold changes. Three-digit IDs denote services, four-digit IDs nodes, five-digit IDs pods; bind an instance through the actual relationship facts. Independent inclusive/exclusive p95 quantiles cannot be subtracted as paired durations. Binned observation order and anomaly-onset summaries do not certify causal order or fault origin. In the required reason, cite actual M/R/L/G field IDs and distinguish measured evidence from inference. Keep the required ranked top-five JSON schema.

Part 4 · text

Exact compact sequences: length/value_positions lists each value and its zero-based bin positions; a-b includes both endpoints. Every bin is present exactly once. Missing/null is no observation, never zero. Metric series references MET01.. bind the same entity, metric and source unit as their metadata.

Part 5 · text

G: calls A -> B means A calls B; hosts node -> pod and owns service -> pod are deployment relations, not causal assertions. Displayed onset is a relative observation time; displayed severity/rank are telemetry summaries.

Part 13 · text

Observed request pairs: all durations below are milliseconds, inclusive span durations. Each pair uses one recorded parent call and its actual direct child in the same request. One longest parent per request/operation, then its longest child per operation; absent children are not zero. Phases use the parent call and the public window. Counts cover eligible captured requests. Slow means strictly above that coordinate's reference p95; it is not a fault label. Associations and duration differences do not identify causal origin, waiting or local execution time.
Pair fields: [id,parent,parent_operation,child,child_operation,[parent_ref_p95,child_ref_p95],phases]. Phase: [name,n,counts[notSlow/notSlow,notSlow/slow,slow/notSlow,slow/slow]].

Prompt：系统、任务、读图及输出要求
You are an expert Site Reliability Engineer performing Root Cause Analysis (RCA) for a microservice application deployed on a Kubernetes cluster.

Identify the original faulty component, not the largest downstream or co-located symptom. Services use three-digit IDs, nodes four, and pods five. Rank exactly five distinct IDs from the exhaustive candidates; lower ranks are alternatives, not five simultaneous causes.

Use the existing SIRCL* discipline in M -> R -> L -> G order:
- M: twelve selected metric series have 64 equal relative-time bins, source units, baseline/peak, signed robust deviation and MET-Z statistics. Compare regular/current means and standard deviations at the public trace-derived split; a shift over 3 sigma is a fluctuating metric. Check the series and missingness against the summary.
- R: operation counts, errors and latency include TRC-L count/exclusive-p95 comparisons. dC/dX are bounded log2 changes; rank_score sums their positive parts. Inclusive duration includes children; explicitly exclusive duration estimates local time after child spans are removed.
- L: Denum-inspired LT template graph bind entity, relative bin, severity, multiplicity and diagnostic numbers. LOG-R compares error-keyword/total-log rates at the same split, surfacing new templates, error bursts and volume drops; check templates and numbers before accepting the score.
- G: directed caller -> callee edges and propagation onset test plausible origins. A symptom in caller A can originate in callee B. Calls, hosting and binned/onset order alone do not establish causality.

Missing/null is absent observation, never zero; times start at the observation window. Keep native units: names/unverified labels do not justify conversions. Metric-z and trace scales differ; log2 ratios are not fold changes. Respect each duration's stated scope; do not subtract independent inclusive/exclusive quantiles as paired durations. Gauges, accumulated counters, restart states and request counts differ. Correlated metrics of one entity are not independent votes. Recovery does not exclude an earlier initiating fault. Bind every claim to the correct entity, metric/operation and visible source ID; distinguish measurements from inference.

Within this single call, form a preliminary top-three, test top-1 with exactly two evidence-answerable checks including a contradiction or alternative origin, then revise if contradicted. Return only the JSON below. Its reason is one compact paragraph, at most three sentences, stating both checks and any decisive relationship. Do not reveal hidden working or output INITIAL/VERIFY/REVISE labels.
{"services":["candidate-id","candidate-id","candidate-id","candidate-id","candidate-id"],"reason":"brief evidence-grounded verification summary","confidence":"high|medium|low"}

Exact compact sequences: length/value_positions lists each value and its zero-based bin positions; a-b includes both endpoints. Every bin is present exactly once. Missing/null is no observation, never zero. Metric series references MET01.. bind the same entity, metric and source unit as their metadata.


G: calls A -> B means A calls B; hosts node -> pod and owns service -> pod are deployment relations, not causal assertions. Displayed onset is a relative observation time; displayed severity/rank are telemetry summaries.


Service names, pod names, and node names are represented by numeric IDs.

Additional observations use the same public reference/current intervals, in seconds from observation start. Medians describe measured samples; inclusive span duration includes child spans. Request counts deduplicate trace IDs and describe captured requests, not unobserved traffic. Calls and hosting are observed relations, not proof of failure propagation.


Additional component facts not already present above. Graph edges are [source,target,relation]; node type follows numeric ID length; isolated nodes are not displayed.


Reference operands AUX01..AUX06 are additional public metric series, supplied identically in all six arms. They use finite canonical samples' median in each of the same64 bins, without interpolation. Their native unit labels are unverified unless explicitly defined; do not infer conversions. Full-window observations: metric series use their original time bins and native units. Extrema and first/last values describe the whole observation window; reference and current intervals retain their public definitions. Trace summaries compare reference/current exclusive p95, not inclusive duration. All original telemetry, additional operands, logs, relations and candidates remain in text.

Part 2 · text

Identify the original faulty component, not the largest downstream or co-located symptom. Services use three-digit IDs, nodes four, and pods five. Rank exactly five distinct IDs from the exhaustive candidates; lower ranks are alternatives, not five simultaneous causes.

Use the existing SIRCL* discipline in M -> R -> L -> G order:
- M: twelve selected metric series have 64 equal relative-time bins, source units, baseline/peak, signed robust deviation and MET-Z statistics. Compare regular/current means and standard deviations at the public trace-derived split; a shift over 3 sigma is a fluctuating metric. Check the series and missingness against the summary.
- R: operation counts, errors and latency include TRC-L count/exclusive-p95 comparisons. dC/dX are bounded log2 changes; rank_score sums their positive parts. Inclusive duration includes children; explicitly exclusive duration estimates local time after child spans are removed.
- L: Denum-inspired LT template graph bind entity, relative bin, severity, multiplicity and diagnostic numbers. LOG-R compares error-keyword/total-log rates at the same split, surfacing new templates, error bursts and volume drops; check templates and numbers before accepting the score.
- G: directed caller -> callee edges and propagation onset test plausible origins. A symptom in caller A can originate in callee B. Calls, hosting and binned/onset order alone do not establish causality.

Missing/null is absent observation, never zero; times start at the observation window. Keep native units: names/unverified labels do not justify conversions. Metric-z and trace scales differ; log2 ratios are not fold changes. Respect each duration's stated scope; do not subtract independent inclusive/exclusive quantiles as paired durations. Gauges, accumulated counters, restart states and request counts differ. Correlated metrics of one entity are not independent votes. Recovery does not exclude an earlier initiating fault. Bind every claim to the correct entity, metric/operation and visible source ID; distinguish measurements from inference.

Within this single call, form a preliminary top-three, test top-1 with exactly two evidence-answerable checks including a contradiction or alternative origin, then revise if contradicted. Return only the JSON below. Its reason is one compact paragraph, at most three sentences, stating both checks and any decisive relationship. Do not reveal hidden working or output INITIAL/VERIFY/REVISE labels.

Prompt：系统、任务、读图及输出要求
You are an expert Site Reliability Engineer performing Root Cause Analysis for a microservice application on Kubernetes.

Diagnose the initiating component using the complete telemetry dashboard. All incident observations and the exhaustive candidate IDs are in the image. Use only its candidate inventory and return exactly five distinct IDs ranked by plausibility; alternatives do not imply simultaneous causes.
Read metrics, traces, logs and observed relationships together. Bind each observation to its original entity, signal/operation, unit and reference/current interval. Native-unit axes are independent unless marked shared. Inclusive span durations include children; only explicitly exclusive latency estimates local time. Do not subtract independent quantiles. A calls B is a directed observation, not proof of causal origin. Co-location, observed onset order and correlated signals alone do not prove a cause. Missing source samples are not zeros; connecting plotted observations creates no additional measurement.
Check the leading candidate against two available observations, including one possible contradiction or alternative origin, within this single call. Return only JSON: {"services":["id1","id2","id3","id4","id5"],"reason":"at most three sentences with evidence references and the checks","confidence":"high|medium|low"}. Do not output intermediate rankings or hidden reasoning.
Identifier transcription rule: The services array contains bare candidate IDs exactly as printed in the card titled "Candidate IDs · exhaustive fixed order". The JSON key services also permits node and pod candidates. Copy each whole ID character for character, including every digit; do not add an entity-type prefix. Card numbers, row indices, axis ticks, measurements and operation-name fragments are not candidate IDs. Before returning JSON, verify that each chosen string occurs in the candidate inventory and that all five strings differ.
Public identity encoding: service IDs have three digits, node IDs four digits and pod IDs five digits, with no leading zero. The services array accepts these three entity types. Copy IDs only from the candidate inventory; a correctly formatted number is not necessarily a candidate.

原请求顺序与全部文本：未经改写的完整 parts

System

You are an expert Site Reliability Engineer performing Root Cause Analysis for a microservice application on Kubernetes.

Part 0 · text

Diagnose the initiating component using the complete telemetry dashboard. All incident observations and the exhaustive candidate IDs are in the image. Use only its candidate inventory and return exactly five distinct IDs ranked by plausibility; alternatives do not imply simultaneous causes.
Read metrics, traces, logs and observed relationships together. Bind each observation to its original entity, signal/operation, unit and reference/current interval. Native-unit axes are independent unless marked shared. Inclusive span durations include children; only explicitly exclusive latency estimates local time. Do not subtract independent quantiles. A calls B is a directed observation, not proof of causal origin. Co-location, observed onset order and correlated signals alone do not prove a cause. Missing source samples are not zeros; connecting plotted observations creates no additional measurement.
Check the leading candidate against two available observations, including one possible contradiction or alternative origin, within this single call. Return only JSON: {"services":["id1","id2","id3","id4","id5"],"reason":"at most three sentences with evidence references and the checks","confidence":"high|medium|low"}. Do not output intermediate rankings or hidden reasoning.
Identifier transcription rule: The services array contains bare candidate IDs exactly as printed in the card titled "Candidate IDs · exhaustive fixed order". The JSON key services also permits node and pod candidates. Copy each whole ID character for character, including every digit; do not add an entity-type prefix. Card numbers, row indices, axis ticks, measurements and operation-name fragments are not candidate IDs. Before returning JSON, verify that each chosen string occurs in the candidate inventory and that all five strings differ.
Public identity encoding: service IDs have three digits, node IDs four digits and pod IDs five digits, with no leading zero. The services array accepts these three entity types. Copy IDs only from the candidate inventory; a correctly formatted number is not necessarily a candidate.
